STATE BANK OF INDIA 
CENTRAL OFFICE 
Bombay, the 20th May 1982 
NOTICE 

SBD. No. 5/82.—In pursuance of sub-scction (2) of Section 
26 of the State Bank of India (Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959 
(38 of 1959), the term of appointment of Dr. A. S. .Cheema, 
20-8/5, Deshbandhu Gupta Road, Karol Bagh, New Delhi, 
nominated as a Director on the Board of the State Bank of 
Patiala in terms of clause (c) of sub-section (1) of Section 
25 of the Act (ibid) expires on the 30th lunc 1982. 

2. Tt is hereby notified for general information that', in 
pursuance of clause (c) of sub-section (1) of Section 25 of 
the Act (ibid), the State Bank of India, in consultation with 
the Reserve Bank of India, has re-nominated Dr, A, S. Cheema 
as a Director on the Board of the State Bank of Patiala for 
a further term of three years from the 1st luly 1982 to 30th 
lunc 1985 (inclusive). 


SBD. No. 6/82,—In pursuance of sub-section (2) of Section 
26 of the State Bank of India (Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959 
(38 of 19,59), the term of appointment of Shri K. N, Barry', 
Chartered Accountant, Messrs. Barry & Company, 1687, Arya 
Samaj Road, New Delhi, nominated as a Director on the 
Board of the State Bank of Patiala in terms of clause (c) 
of sub-section (1) of Section 25 of the Act (ibid) expires 
on the 30th lune 1982. 

2, Tt is hereby notified for general information that, in 
pursuance of clause (c) of sub-section (1) of Section 25 of 
the Act (ibid), the State Bank of Tndia, in consultation with 
the Reserve Bank of India, has re-nominated Shri K. N. 
Barry as a Director on the Board of the State Bank of Patiala 
for a further term of three years from the 1st July 1982 to 
30th June 1985 (inclusive). 

P. C. D. NAMBTAR, 
Chairman
